Somewhere behind the glass, a man shouted that the lower stairs had been blocked. The guard looked from the soil to the covered cart.","Cassian did not wait. He drove the cart forward, caught the gate before it closed, and held it open with his shoulder. Noor pushed from the other side. Peta pulled Mara back as the guard reached for her arm.","Mara twisted out of his grasp, stepped on his foot with her muddy shoe, and swept the loose hose from its rack. Water struck the pavement between them. The guard slipped, caught the fence, and swore.","\"The return,\" she told him, \"is evidently leaking.\"","She ran.","June had both rear doors open. Ivo and Rowan lifted Elena's chair into the adapted van while Noor climbed in beside her. Mara reached the step just as Harrow appeared at the loading door.","\"Stop that vehicle!\"","Cassian caught Mara around the waist and swung her inside. She landed against his chest. The van moved before he had closed the door.","\"We need to stop making this a habit,\" he said.","\"You first.\"","He kissed the top of her head with fierce relief, then reached past her to fasten the chair's safety strap.","Noor checked Elena's breathing. The older woman lay back under the blanket, exhausted but watching the ceiling lights.","\"Outside?\" she asked.","Mara took her hand. \"Outside.\"","The single word filled more space than any victory speech could have done.","The security car followed them for three streets. June did not race it. She drove towards the hospital district, where traffic, street cameras, and late pedestrians made a quiet interception less convenient. Rowan called an independent patient-transfer service from the front seat. He gave the location and the patient's immediate needs, then stayed on the line.","Behind them, the security car turned away.","Mara looked through the rear window until it disappeared. Her knees had become numb. She pressed her face into her mother's blanket and discovered, with a shock, that she was shaking.","Elena's fingers moved over her hair. \"You're tired.\"","Mara laughed into the fabric. \"You too.\"","Mercy House opened its side entrance for them. The hospital's old architecture seemed impossibly gentle after the glass and steel below the conservatory. A nurse wheeled Elena towards a quiet room while Noor walked beside her, explaining what she had observed and what remained unknown.","The room had a window overlooking a small courtyard. Somebody had planted cabbage roses around the fountain. Their pink heads shone beneath a security light.","Elena watched them for a long time.","\"Real weather,\" she said.","Mara sat beside the bed. Cassian brought a jug of water and put it where Elena could reach without stretching. Mrs. Wren, summoned by a message nobody remembered sending, arrived with a soft shawl and a bowl of soup.","She looked at Elena, then at Mara. Her face changed.","\"Well,\" she said, in the voice she used when something mattered too much for fuss. \"We will need another chair.\"","Mara began to cry.","She had held herself together through the gate, the chair lift, and the pursuit. An extra chair defeated her completely. Mrs. Wren set down the soup and drew her into a brisk, fierce embrace.","Across the bed, Elena closed her eyes. A tear slid towards her ear.","Elena watched Cassian carry the extra chair in. He tested its legs before setting it beside the bed, then moved the lamp so its glare would not fall across her eyes.","\"Does he always do that?\" she asked Mara.","\"Interfere with the furniture? Frequently.\"","\"Notice.\"","Mara looked at him. \"More than he thinks I notice.\"","Cassian, who had plainly heard, found it necessary to straighten the shawl at the foot of the bed. Mrs. Wren made no attempt to hide her fondness.","Elena held out her hand to him. \"Thank you for bringing her.\"","He took it gently. \"She brought us.\"","Her mother's smile trembled. \"Yes. She would.\"","Mara sat very still. She had spent so long preparing to defend herself that being known correctly, without a speech, seemed a privilege too delicate to move around. Rowan came in with a bag of overnight things and immediately dropped it against the doorway. The ordinary noise rescued her from tears.","\"Shoes,\" he announced. \"Several pairs. I decided the expedition deserved a wholesale replacement.\"","\"Did you buy the right size?\" June asked.","\"Certainly not. I relied on intuition and a persuasive cashier.\"","Mara laughed. Elena looked from one face to another as though slowly recognising the outline of a life she had missed. Nobody tried to soften that loss with a promise that it had all been for the best. They made space around her bed, passed the tea, and stayed.","Later, when the room had settled, Noor asked for a little space to examine her. Mara went into the corridor with Cassian. She carried the repaired flower in one hand and the small loose petal in the other.","He drew her against him. She rested her forehead on his chest and listened to the ordinary beat beneath his coat.","\"She's here,\" she said.","\"Yes.\"","\"I keep expecting somebody to tell me I made a mistake.\"","He held her more closely. \"We have time to check everything that needs checking. You don't have to stop being glad while we do it.\"","She looked up. His face was tired, the scar above his eyebrow still marked from the storm. She wanted, suddenly, a room with no alarms and a morning that belonged to them.","\"When this is over,\" she said, \"I'm stealing you for a day.\"","\"A day?\"","\"Perhaps several. Your availability will influence the quantity.\"","He brushed his thumb across her cheek. \"My answer is yes. You can write the schedule later.\"","The kiss was slow this time. No corridor door interrupted them. Mara held the front of his coat and let herself have it, warmth and hunger and all the awkward joy of wanting something that wanted her back.","June appeared at the far end of the passage, saw them, and silently reversed direction. A moment later they heard her tell Rowan that the corridor was closed for structural repairs.","Mara laughed against Cassian's mouth. He looked as though he might never recover his composure.","The hospital adviser found them after midnight. She had spoken to Holt and would arrange independent identification and a review of Elena's records. Elena could state that she wanted to remain at Mercy House. Any challenge to that decision would have to be addressed openly, not through another unmarked vehicle.","Mara signed the visitor register with a hand that was finally steady. She wrote DAUGHTER in the relationship field.","At the next table, Ivo had arranged Peta's copied records beside the cable photographs. The communications rack's station log showed the same contact-state changes Mara had filmed. Rowan had obtained the product description attached to the spiral emblem. It referred to synchronized terminal scheduling.","\"A network,\" Ivo said. \"It sends instructions to equipment. It doesn't send someone's whole mind down a wire.\"","Noor joined them, closing Elena's door softly. \"She's sleeping. We stop asking her questions tonight.\"","Nobody argued.","Peta pointed to a return line on the plan. \"The carriers were developed in the lower unit. They are introduced into the closed trial-water circuit. Maintenance can connect that circuit to the public supply. I reported the cross-connection.\"","\"And the contact nodes?\" Cassian asked.","\"Still required. The circuit is part of a delivery and calibration system, not a transmitter to every person who drinks water.\"","Mara looked at the photographs of the cuff and bench. The underground machine had become less magical and more frightening: it needed doors, cables, money, and people willing to treat another person as a setting.","Her phone rang.","Harrow's name filled the screen. She let it continue until he left a message.","\"Miss Vale. You have removed a patient and proprietary materials from a clinical facility. We can settle this quietly if you return both before morning.\"","Mara listened once, then saved it.","Cassian's expression hardened. \"You should leave the district for a few days. Take your mother somewhere—\"","\"No.\"","He stopped.","\"She has a room, a doctor, and people who know where she is,\" Mara said. \"I'm not taking her back into a hidden route. It flew across the inlet, low and fast, until its shape became part of the sky.","The boy cheered. He wanted to go back to work and bring something home. She understood that hunger better than any graph.","At Mercy House, Ewan was awake.
